Rubric-based judges offer a transparent way to decompose such judgments into explicit evaluation criteria, but existing annotation-free rubric generators typically rely on a single generic evaluator. As a result, they may overlook important dimensions of human preference, a failure mode we term dimensional blind spots. To address this limitation, we propose Multi-Role Rubr","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-02T07:50:38Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2607_01830.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文关注开放式任务中的 judge 和 reward 信号构造问题：单个通用 evaluator 自动生成 rubric 时，经常漏掉人类偏好的某些维度，导致打分在不同领域不稳定，也给模型留下针对 rubric 的投机空间。","importance":"这篇论文更偏 post-training 和 evaluation，但对预训练研究仍有间接价值。预训练之后的 SFT、RLHF、RLVR 都依赖 reward 或 preference 信号；如果信号只覆盖部分质量维度，模型会学习到狭窄目标，甚至放大 reward hacking。MRRG 的启发是，reward 质量不只来自更强的 judge model，也来自评价维度是否完整、是否可审计。对研究 pretrain-posttrain 接口的人来说，它提供了一个低成本构造可解释 reward 的方案。","technical_approach":"方法名为 Multi-Role Rubric Generation，简称 MRRG。它不训练新的 reward model，也不需要参考答案或人工标注。给定输入 prompt 后，系统让同一个 LLM 扮演多个互补评价角色，例如 user、domain expert、educator、AI researcher、linguist。每个角色生成短小、原子化、可验证的 rubric item。随后系统把这些 item 汇总、去重，形成一个可审计的 rubric scorer。评分时，LLM 按每条 criterion 对候选回答打分，再聚合成标量 reward。这个 scorer 既可用于 pairwise preference validation，也可直接作为 GRPO-style RLVR 的 reward。","experiments":"实验在 RewardBench-2、JudgeBench 和 PPE 上验证 judge 质量，覆盖 Qwen2.5 和 GPT 系列模型。论文报告 MRRG 相比最强列出 baseline 有 3.1 到 16.4 个百分点提升。用于 GRPO-style RLVR 时，MRRG 相比最强 single-voice reward baseline 在 BiGGen Bench 上提升 1.7 分，在 HealthBench-Hard 上提升 3.4 分。引言中的 NASDAQ 示例展示了 single-voice rubric 容易奖励表面完成度，却漏掉可行性、认知校准和不可验证声明等维度。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 20 到 30 分钟。先看 Introduction 中 dimensional blind spots 和 rubric hacking 的定义，以及 Figure 1、Table 1 的失败案例。方法部分重点看 2.2 到 2.4：多角色如何生成 rubric，如何去重聚合，如何作为 judge 和 GRPO reward 共用。实验部分看 RewardBench-2、JudgeBench、PPE 的总体表格，以及 RLVR 部分是否真的改善下游 reward optimization。若只关心预训练，可跳过大部分 prompt 细节，保留对 reward 信号覆盖面的理解。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies how to build reliable rubric-based judges and reward signals for open-ended LLM outputs without human annotations or reference answers. Its central claim is that single generic rubric generators miss important preference dimensions, creating unstable judging accuracy and opportunities for rubric hacking.","importance":"This is mainly a post-training and evaluation paper, but it is relevant to the pretrain-to-posttrain interface. RLHF and RLVR depend on the quality of the reward signal. If the rubric covers only a narrow slice of human preference, optimization can improve the measured score while degrading unmeasured qualities. The useful idea here is that reward quality can be improved by broadening and auditing the evaluation dimensions, not only by using a stronger judge model.","technical_approach":"MRRG, or Multi-Role Rubric Generation, is a training-free and reference-free framework. For each prompt, an LLM is asked to generate evaluation criteria from several complementary roles, such as user, domain expert, educator, AI researcher, and linguist. Each role proposes compact, atomic, and verifiable rubric items. These items are then pooled and deduplicated into a rubric-based scorer. At scoring time, the judge evaluates a candidate response against each criterion and aggregates the results into a scalar score. The same scorer can be used for pairwise preference validation and as a reward in GRPO-style RLVR.","experiments":"The paper evaluates MRRG on RewardBench-2, JudgeBench, and PPE using Qwen2.5 and GPT-family models. The introductory examples show the failure mode clearly: a single-voice rubric can reward surface-level task completion while missing feasibility, epistemic calibration, and unverifiable claims.","reading_guide":"A 20 to 30 minute skim is enough for most pretraining researchers. This guides attribution, capability knockout, and model pruning downstream to operate by scoring each unit by the effect of ablation in isolation. Such first-order scoring is natural when component importance is additive, but becomes misleading when a transformer self-repairs: after a primary component is removed, a dormant backup can take over, muting the primary's measured ef","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-02T09:32:57Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_01940.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在 Transformer 机制可解释性研究中，一阶消融打分（单独消融一个组件并度量输出变化）假设组件重要性是可加的，但模型存在 self-repair 现象：主路径被移除后，休眠的备份组件会接管功能，导致主组件的重要性被低估，备份组件在完整模型上又看似无关。这种非可加性使归因、能力敲除、结构化剪枝等下游任务都偏向冗余组件。","importance":"对预训练研究的价值在于：它指出了当前基于单组件消融的归因、剪枝、电路发现方法的一个系统性盲区——冗余备份路径。对于理解大模型能力的鲁棒性来源、设计更可靠的剪枝/蒸馏策略、以及通过 circuit-level 分析指导预训练架构设计都有参考意义。CoAx 作为一种无监督、输出驱动的二阶打分方法，补充了一阶发现方法的不足。","technical_approach":"提出条件协同消融（CoAx），将 self-repair 重新表述为「条件电路补全」任务：给定一个候选主电路集合 S，度量每个剩余组件的消融效果在 S 被移除后的增长量。休眠备份组件单独消融时效果小，但条件消融时效果大；无关组件则两者都小。CoAx 基于 Fisher 加权的消融能量增长来打分，使用单次消融基线和一次条件前向传播即可计算，无需标签。本质上是读取了一阶可加打分丢弃的二阶交互项。","experiments":"在 GPT-2-small 的 IOI 任务上，CoAx 将备份注意力头识别的 ROC-AUC 从 0.33 提升到 0.91，优于包括 self-repair-aware 的 AtP* GradDrop（最佳 0.82）。反事实 patching 确认恢复的备份头确实承担了修复功能。同一无监督流程可迁移到 8 个不同模型的 induction 任务。恢复备份后，被 self-repair 掩盖的归因信号从 0.22 logit-difference 恢复到 1.76；能力敲除准确率达到 0.70，接近文档记录的 oracle 0.72；结构化剪枝效果从 124M 扩展到 7B 参数模型。","reading_guide":"重点阅读 Section 2（CoAx 形式化定义和 Fisher 度量的推导），理解为什么条件视角能暴露非可加重要性。Section 3 的 IOI 备份头验证实验和 induction 跨模型迁移实验展示了方法的有效性。Section 4 的下游应用（归因修正、敲除、剪枝）展示了实际价值。值得深挖的点：CoAx 的计算成本随主集合 S 的选择如何变化；Fisher 度量相比直接 logit 差别的优势；以及这种条件视角能否扩展到更细粒度的 feature-level 分析（而非 head-level）。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"In mechanistic interpretability of Transformers, first-order ablation scoring (ablating a single component and measuring output change) assumes additive component importance. But models exhibit self-repair: when a primary path is removed, dormant backup components take over, causing the primary's importance to be under-measured while backups appear irrelevant on the intact model. This non-additivity biases downstream tasks like attribution, capability knockout, and structured pruning against redundant components.","importance":"For pretraining research, this work exposes a systematic blind spot in current component-ablation-based attribution, pruning, and circuit discovery methods—redundant backup paths. It informs our understanding of where model robustness comes from, suggests more reliable pruning/distillation strategies, and provides a circuit-level lens that could guide pretraining architecture design. CoAx, as an unsupervised, output-grounded second-order scoring method, complements first-order discovery approaches.","technical_approach":"Conditional Co-Ablation (CoAx) reframes self-repair as a 'conditional circuit completion' task: given a candidate primary set S, measure how much each remaining unit's ablation effect grows once S is removed. A dormant backup has small standalone effect but large conditional effect; an irrelevant unit has neither. CoAx scores via Fisher-weighted ablation energy growth, computed from a single-ablation baseline and one conditional forward pass. It is label-free and essentially reads the second-order interaction that additive node scores discard.","experiments":"On GPT-2-small IOI, CoAx raises backup-head identification ROC-AUC from 0.33 to 0.91, outperforming baselines including self-repair-aware AtP* GradDrop (best 0.82). Counterfactual patching confirms recovered heads causally carry the repair. The same label-free procedure transfers to induction across 8 models. After recovery, self-repair-masked attribution signal grows from 0.22 to 1.76 logit-difference; capability knockout reaches 0.70 accuracy, near the documented oracle of 0.72; structured head pruning improves and scales from 124M to 7B parameters.","reading_guide":"Focus on Section 2 for the formalization of CoAx and the Fisher metric derivation—this is where the conditional perspective that exposes non-additive importance is laid out. Section 3's IOI backup head validation and cross-model induction transfer demonstrate effectiveness. Section 4's downstream applications (attribution correction, knockout, pruning) show practical value. Worth digging into: how CoAx's cost scales with the choice of primary set S; the advantage of Fisher metric over direct logit-difference; and whether this conditional lens extends to feature-level rather than head-level analysis.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读方法和 IOI 实验即可；它提醒剪枝/归因别只信单点消融，backup circuit 会系统性藏起来。","en":"Read the method and IOI experiment; it is a useful warning that pruning and attribution should not trust isolated ablations when backup circuits exist."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zhiren Gong","Zihao Zeng","Chau Yuen","Wei Yang Bryan Lim"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-interpretability","transformer-circuits","ablation","self-repair","gpt-2"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01940","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.01940","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.01774","title":"Subliminal Clocks: Latent Time Modelling in Diffusion Language Models","abstract":"Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models. Unlike standard diffusion-based approaches, DLMs are not explicitly conditioned on a timestep, raising a natural question: do these models internally represent denoising progress, and how is such information used downstream? In this work, we show that DLMs do in fact encode a latent representation related to the diffusion timestep within their residual streams. We find that this signal can","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-02T06:45:42Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_01774.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究的是：masked diffusion language model 在推理时虽然没有像连续扩散模型那样显式输入 timestep，但模型内部是否仍然形成了“当前去噪进度”的隐式表征，以及这个表征是否真的参与了生成控制。作者把去噪进度具体化为当前序列中已解 mask 的比例 τ，并把问题拆成三步：能否从残差流中读出 τ；能否沿着对应方向做因果干预；这个时间信号在不同层里呈现什么几何结构。这个问题不直接提升指标，但它触及 DLM 的内部计算机制：模型到底是在做纯局部填空，还是在维护一个全局的 generation state。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要在机制层面，而不是 recipe 层面。第一，它给 masked diffusion LM 提供了一个较清楚的内部状态变量：去噪进度不是外部分析者强加的观测量，而是模型残差流里可解码、可操控的信号。第二，它说明 time-agnostic 的 DLM 并不等于“没有时间”，而是把时间编码成了隐变量；这对后续设计显式时间控制、分层去噪策略、step-adaptive compute、以及 any-order / masked LM 的统一理解都有启发。第三，作者做了 probe 之外的 steering 和几何分析，至少部分避免了“能线性读出不代表被使用”的老问题。不过这篇工作的贡献量级仍偏 interpretability：它不会直接改变当前主流预训练流程，更像是在为 DLM 建模范式补一块机制拼图。","technical_approach":"方法上有三层。第一层是恢复信号。作者在 LLaDA-1.5 和 Dream 上，对每一层残差流 hidden state 训练单独的 MLP probe，回归当前推理步的 τ。probe 分别只看 mask token、只看非 mask token、或看全部 token，用来判断时间信息是否集中在某类位置。文中结果显示，多数中后层都能稳定恢复 τ，且 R² 在相当宽的层范围内保持较高，说明这不是某一层偶然泄漏的局部特征。\n\n第二层是因果操控。作者不是只停留在线性可读性，而是从 probe 学到的时间相关子空间中提取低维方向，对残差流做 steering，观察模型生成时“主观时间”的变化。直观上，如果把 hidden state 往“更晚的去噪阶段”方向推，模型会表现得更愿意提交具体 token、减少保守性；往“更早阶段”方向推，则更像仍处在高不确定度的去噪早期。这个设计的关键在于：它不改 sampling schedule，也不改外部 mask ratio，而是只改内部表征，因此更接近检验“模型是否真的用到了这个内部时间变量”。\n\n第三层是几何分析。作者考察不同层中时间信号的可分离性、维度和对齐关系，试图回答它是分散编码还是集中在低维子空间。摘要和正文都强调是 low-dimensional subspace，这意味着 τ 不是弥散在整个表示空间里的弱统计痕迹，而更像一个可识别的全局控制轴。若这一点成立，它和很多 AR 模型里出现的全局控制变量、phase signal、甚至 layerwise computation stage 有相通之处。\n\n和已有工作相比，这篇文章的区别在于它不研究 attention sink、mask 操纵攻击或 SAE 概念 steering，而是直接盯住 DLM 最核心但此前没被明确刻画的内部变量：denoising progress。它也不同于理论上把 mask ratio 与连续时间对应起来的工作；这里研究的是模型是否真的在内部实现了这种对应。","experiments":"实验主体建立在 LLaDA-1.5 和 Dream 两个代表性 masked DLM 上。可从正文读到的关键信息是：\n\n1. Probe 结果：在 LLaDA 上，按层训练的 probe 在较大范围内都能达到 R² > 0.5，说明 τ 从残差流中是稳定可恢复的，而不是只能在输入层或输出层读到。作者还比较了只用 mask token、只用非 mask token、以及全 token 的设置，用来判断时间信号的空间分布。这个设计很重要，因为如果只有 mask 位携带 τ，说明它可能只是显式观测量的重编码；如果非 mask 位也强可读，则更支持“全局状态”解释。\n\n2. Steering 结果：沿时间子空间做干预后，模型的去噪行为会系统性偏移。虽然摘要截断，没有给出完整数字，但正文主张这种调制是 systematic 的，而不是少量样例上的现象学展示。阅读时应重点确认作者是否报告了：接受率/解 mask 速度/输出熵/最终文本质量等量化变化，以及 steering 强度与行为变化是否近似单调。\n\n3. 几何结果：作者分析了时间信号在层间的组织方式，结论是存在低维结构。这里最值得看的是：低维到什么程度；不同层的时间方向是否对齐；是否存在中层最强、后层回落的 pattern；LLaDA 与 Dream 是否一致。如果两个模型都出现相似层结构，这个现象就更像范式属性，而不是单模型偶然性。\n\n从预训练研究者角度，这篇文章的实验强项是把“可读出—可操控—有几何结构”串成了闭环；弱项是它主要是分析型工作，缺少把这一发现转成更好训练目标或更高效推理策略的后续验证。","reading_guide":"建议先看 Introduction 里三个研究问题和 τ 的定义，再看 Recovering τ 这一节的 probe 设定与图 2。这里决定了整篇文章是否站得住：如果 probe 只是读到 mask 比例的表面统计，后面的解释会变弱。阅读时要特别留意作者如何控制 trivial cues，比如 token identity、位置、当前 mask 分布。\n\n第二个重点是 steering 部分。要看清楚时间子空间是怎么构造的，是 probe 梯度、线性回归方向、还是某种子空间分解；干预发生在单层还是多层；是一次性加偏置还是逐步注入。这里关系到因果解释的力度。\n\n第三个重点是几何分析和附录。附录里通常会给出 probe 架构、训练细节、不同 token 子集、以及 τ 与连续时间 s 的关系推导。若只想快速判断价值，读主文图 2、steering 主图、以及层间几何图，大约 25 分钟够用。若想把它作为 DLM 机制分析的参考模板，建议连附录一起看 60 分钟左右。\n\n值得深挖的点有两个：一是这个 latent clock 是否能用于 step-adaptive decoding，例如让模型自己决定何时该更激进地 unmask；二是它是否只是 mask ratio 的函数，还是还编码了“剩余不确定度”这类更丰富的状态量。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a precise mechanistic question about masked diffusion language models: even though these models are not explicitly conditioned on a timestep at inference, do they internally represent denoising progress, and is that representation actually used by the model? The authors operationalize denoising progress as the fraction of unmasked tokens, τ, and study three sub-questions: whether τ can be decoded from residual-stream activations, whether intervening on the corresponding direction causally changes generation behavior, and how this signal is organized across layers. This is not a benchmark-chasing paper; it is about whether DLMs maintain an internal global state rather than merely performing local masked-token recovery.","importance":"Its value is mainly conceptual and mechanistic. First, it provides evidence that time-agnostic masked DLMs are not truly “without time”; they appear to encode denoising progress as a latent internal variable. Second, that matters for how one thinks about diffusion-style language modeling more broadly: explicit timestep conditioning may be absent at the interface while an implicit clock still emerges in the representation. Third, the paper goes beyond pure probing by adding steering and geometric analysis, which makes the claim stronger than a simple linear-readout result. The practical impact on pretraining recipes is limited for now, but the work is relevant if one cares about DLM internals, adaptive denoising schedules, controllable generation, or the broader question of what global state variables language models learn to maintain.","technical_approach":"$33","experiments":"$34","reading_guide":"$35","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"扩散语言模型存在内部时间表征的发现值得关注，做非AR建模范式研究可重点看第3节探针实验与第4节调控实验。","en":"The discovery of latent timestep representations in diffusion LMs is noteworthy; non-AR paradigm researchers should focus on Section 3 probing and Section 4 steering experiments."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Maximo Rulli","Thomas Fontanari","Simone Petruzzi","Federico Alvetreti","Giorgio Strano","Donato Crisostomi","Giorgos Nikolaou","Tommaso Mencattini","Andrea Santilli","Emanuele Rodolà","Simone Scardapane","Alessio Devoto"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["diffusion language models","interpretability","latent representation","denoising progress","probing"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01774","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2607.01774","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2607.02396","title":"Fast Multi-dimensional Refusal Subspaces via RFM-AGOP","abstract":"Steering and monitoring activations in Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used for both safety and interpretability. However, existing methods for extracting these subspaces are computationally expensive, which becomes prohibitive on reasoning models who produce long reasoning trace","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-02T16:31:56Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_02396.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"提取LLM中控制有害query拒绝行为的多维子空间时，现有方法计算成本过高，在生成长度可达数千token的推理模型上实用性差；此前基于RFM-AGOP的拒绝分析仅使用top-1特征向量，单一线性方向不足以刻画大模型中拒绝行为的多维结构。","importance":"给出了秒级速度的多维拒绝子空间提取方案，可用于表征工程、模型安全干预、对齐机制分析，尤其适用于长思维链模型上的安全表征研究，无需运行昂贵的梯度优化过程即可快速定位子空间开展消融或干预实验。属于实用工具类贡献，对基座预训练的直接启发有限。","technical_approach":"对RFM-AGOP框架做两处稳定性改进，适配多维子空间提取任务。第一，将原框架的单位矩阵初始化替换为探针引导初始化：先训练简单线性探针区分有害/无害query的激活，用探针权重向量加激活的秩k截断协方差构造初始特征矩阵，解决原方法对超参数敏感、早期迭代容易偏离主方向的问题。第二，在AGOP矩阵迭代更新时加入指数移动平均，降低更新噪声。收敛后取最终特征矩阵的top-k特征向量张成拒绝子空间，全程不需要梯度优化。方法采用马哈拉诺比斯拉普拉斯核，迭代执行核岭回归与AGOP更新。","experiments":"在Qwen2.5（非推理模型）和Qwen3（推理模型，最长思维链达8000 token）上开展实验，子空间提取耗时仅数秒，速度显著高于此前的拒绝锥优化（RCO）方法。实验验证单一线性方向确实无法完整刻画拒绝行为：多维子空间在两类操作上效果更好，一是将激活投影到子空间正交补以消除拒绝行为，二是沿基向量方向缩放以引导模型拒绝或回答有害query。正文未报告具体的拒绝率、能力保留数值。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，重点关注方法部分的两处改动（探针初始化、EMA更新）和实验中关于多维子空间必要性的对比，预计阅读时间15分钟。无需深究RFM的理论推导，了解该工具可快速提取多维行为子空间即可；如需复现，再查阅附录中数据集构建、层选择的细节。","authors_note":"作者来自Télécom SudParis、ENS Paris-Saclay，工作发表于ICML 2026机制可解释性研讨会。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Extracting the multi-dimensional refusal subspace — internal representations that govern LLM refusal of harmful queries — is computationally expensive with existing methods, making the process impractical for reasoning models that produce reasoning traces thousands of tokens long. Prior RFM-AGOP work on refusal analysis only uses the top-1 eigenvector, and a single linear direction cannot fully capture refusal structure in larger models.","importance":"The method extracts multi-dimensional refusal subspaces in seconds, making it usable for representation engineering, safety intervention, and alignment mechanism analysis — especially for safety studies on long-CoT reasoning models, as it eliminates the need for expensive gradient-based optimization to locate subspaces for ablation or steering. It is a practical tooling contribution, with limited direct relevance to base model pretraining.","technical_approach":"Two stability modifications are made to the RFM-AGOP framework to support multi-dimensional subspace extraction. First, the original identity matrix initialization is replaced with a probe-informed starting point: a simple linear probe is trained to separate activations from harmful and harmless queries, and the initial feature matrix is constructed from the probe weight vector plus the rank-k truncated covariance of activations. This fixes the original RFM’s sensitivity to hyperparameters and tendency to diverge from the primary direction in early iterations. Second, exponential moving average is applied to AGOP matrix updates to reduce iteration noise. After convergence, the top-k eigenvectors of the final feature matrix span the refusal subspace; no gradient optimization is required through the whole process. The method uses a Mahalanobis Laplace kernel, and iterates between kernel ridge regression and AGOP updates.","experiments":"Evaluations are run on Qwen 2.5 (non-reasoning) and Qwen 3 (reasoning model, with CoT up to 8000 tokens). Subspace extraction takes only seconds, substantially faster than prior Refusal Cone Optimization (RCO). Results confirm a single linear direction cannot fully model refusal behavior: the multi-dimensional subspace performs better on both standard operations — projecting activations to the orthogonal complement of the subspace to remove refusal, and scaling along basis vectors to steer the model toward refusal or compliance. Exact refusal rate and capability retention numbers are not reported in the main text.","reading_guide":"Skim for the two core method modifications (probe initialization, EMA update) and the empirical comparison supporting the need for multi-dimensional subspaces; expect around 15 minutes of reading. There is no need to dig into RFM theoretical derivations unless implementing the method — it is sufficient to know this tool can rapidly extract multi-dimensional behavioral subspaces. Refer to appendices for dataset construction and layer selection details only for reproduction.","authors_note":"Authors are affiliated with Télécom SudParis and ENS Paris-Saclay. This inference problem closely resembles the canonical setup of compressed sensing, but dense decoders requires $O(mn)$ learned values, which becomes costly at large feature counts.","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-02T07:16:14Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2607_01799.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.IT"],"summary":{"core_problem":"现有稀疏自编码器（SAE）用于大模型机制可解释性时，稠密解码器的参数量随特征维度与激活维度的乘积增长，存储与推理成本过高，且现有SAE变体均未针对解码器的存储约束做结构化优化。","importance":"为SAE的存储-保真度权衡提供了新的结构化稀疏方案，降低了3B以上规模大模型可解释性分析的资源门槛；将组合压缩感知的扩展图理论引入SAE设计，为稀疏特征的可识别性提供了理论支撑，也为后续把结构化稀疏方法应用于预训练模型内部表示拆解提供了参考。","technical_approach":"核心是提出Expander SAE，用左d正则二部扩展图的邻接矩阵作为固定掩码，约束绑定权重的TopK SAE的编码器和解码器的权重非零位置——每个解码器列只有d个非零值（d远小于激活维度m），把可学习参数量从O(mn)降到O(dn)。核心贡献包括三点：1）架构上实现字典质量与摊销推理质量的解耦；2）提出适配列稀疏结构的并行正交匹配追踪（OMP）实现，在摊销推理速度和迭代解码保真度之间形成帕累托最优前沿；3）从理论上证明：当掩码满足2k特征子集扩展条件、且学习到的解码器列在其支撑集上足够平坦时，任意无噪k稀疏编码具有唯一的k稀疏解释；在更强的累积相干条件下，OMP可精确恢复稀疏支撑集。与现有SAE变体（Gated、JumpReLU、TopK等）仅修改编码器稀疏机制、保持解码器稠密的设计不同，Expander decoders in existing sparse autoencoders (SAEs) incur O(mn) parameter cost that scales prohibitively with feature count, creating excessive storage and inference overhead when applying SAEs to sparsity approach that expands the SAE storage-fidelity tradeoff frontier, lowering the resource barrier for interpretability analysis of 3B+ parameter models. It also bridges combinatorial compressed sensing theory to SAE design, providing formal identifiability guarantees for sparse features and informing future work on structured decomposition of pretrained model internal representations.","technical_approach":"$36","experiments":"Validated on four open-source models (Pythia-70M/160M, Qwen2.5-3B, Llama-3.2-1B). best-of-n 排序、提前停止或动态分配 test-time compute。它不是提升预训练损失的工作，但能帮助理解 reasoning model 的内部动态。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把固定层的 hidden states 序列形式化为曲线 γ=(h1,...,hn)，再定义几类 geometric functionals。谱特征关注轨迹点云的协方差或 或文本模式的方法不同，它直接读模型内部表征；与一般 probing 不同，它关心的是生成过程中形成的整条轨迹，而不是单个 token 或单层静态表示。","experiments":"关键结果有两个。第一，仅用有效维度相关特征区分数学题 easy/hard，AUC 超过 0.93，说明谱平坦度和任务难度之间有较强相关。第二，只使用生成前 20% token 的七个运动学和位置特征预测最终解答正确性，AUC 达到 0.806。这对 best-of-n 和早停比较有意义：如果早期轨迹已经显示某条推理路径质量较差，可以减少继续生成的预算。需要注意的是，结果主要支持相关性和可预测性，还不能说明有效维度就是推理难度的因果机制；不同模型层、采样温度和题型分布下的稳定性需要进一步检查。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20–30 分钟。重点看 is mechanistic rather than a new pretraining recipe. It treats CoT generation as a discrete curve in representation space and characterizes that curve with spectral, positional, and kinematic features. For foundation-model research, the useful point is that task hardness may correspond to broader exploration of hidden dimensions, and early trajectory geometry may help rank best-of-n samples, trigger early stopping, or allocate test-time compute more selectively.","technical_approach":"For a fixed transformer layer, the generated hidden states are modeled as a curve γ=(h1,...,hn). The paper then defines geometric functionals over this curve. The spectral side analyzes the eigenvalue profile of the trajectory covariance or related point-cloud structure. The effective dimension dρ measures how many latent directions the trajectory occupies. The proposed interpretation is simple: easy problems produce more concentrated trajectories with a few dominant directions, while harder problems have flatter spectra and spread across more hidden dimensions. The paper also uses positional and kinematic statistics, such as displacement and velocity/acceleration-like changes, to predict whether a generated solution will be correct. This differs from token-level heuristics such as CoT length or entropy because it uses the model’s internal trajectory, not only the output text.","experiments":"The main reported numbers are strong for a probing-style analysis. Seven positional and kinematic features computed from only the first 20% of generated tokens predict final correctness with AUC 0.806. Robustness across layers, models, sampling temperatures, and task distributions is the key open question.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough. Retention collapses because exi","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-07-01T22:38:54Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2607_01523.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究 recurrent memory agent 在超长上下文下为什么会失效。核心发现是：主要瓶颈不是模型没有把关键信息写进 memory，而是写进去之后在后续反复更新中被覆盖掉。作者把问题拆成 memory capture 和 memory retention 两个环节，指出 retention 才是随上下文长度增长而快速退化的环节。","importance":"对长上下文和 agent memory 研究有直接启发。它没有改模型、不开长窗口、也不训练新的 memory module，而是从 memory 组织结构入手，说明固定大小文本 memory 的主要风险来自单块 memory 的反复覆盖。这个结论对预训练研究的启发在于：长上下文能力不只由 context window 或位置编码决定，外部 recurrent compression 的结构也会形成明确的信息保留瓶颈。它更偏 inference-time memory architecture，但对设计长程信息压缩任务、评测长上下文 retention failure 有参考价值。","technical_approach":"作者先定义两个指标：Memory Capture Rate 衡量答案信息是否曾经出现在任一中间 memory 状态中；Memory Retention Rate 衡量已经捕获的信息是否能保留到最终 memory。基于这个诊断，提出 Multi-Head Recurrent Memory。传统 recurrent memory 把所有信息维护在一个文本块里，每次 update 都同时读旧 memory 和新 chunk，再生成新的 memory，因此任何更新都可能覆盖早期信息。MHM 把总 memory 划分为多个独立 head，每一步只选择一个 head 更新，其余 head 保持不变。这样未被选中的 head 在当前 step 不承受覆盖风险。具体实现 MHM-LRU 使用 least-recently-updated 规则选择 head，不依赖训练，也不需要额外 token 预算。单头 recurrent memory 可以看成 MHM 的退化情形。","experiments":"实验重点放在极长上下文下的 retention 和最终任务准确率。作者在 RULER-HQA 等长上下文基准上测试 100K 到 1M token 范围。诊断实验显示，代表性方法 MemAgent 的 capture 在不同长度下相对稳定，但 retention 随长度增长明显下降，在 896K token 时低于 30%。MHM-LRU 在同一设置下把 RULER-HQA 896K token 的 memory retention rate 提升到 73.96%。最终准确率也随 retention 改善而更稳定，baseline 随上下文变长明显退化。作者还报告了跨模型家族、模型规模和任务类型的泛化结果，说明收益不是单一 prompt 或单一模型的偶然现象。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，预计 20 到 30 分钟。优先看 failure characterization 部分，尤其是 数量或更新策略相关消融。无需细读 neuroscience 类比和较泛的 agent 背景叙述。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks why recurrent-memory agents fail as the input context becomes extremely long. Its main diagnosis is that the dominant failure mode is not failing to write the relevant evidence into memory, but failing to keep it through later memory updates.","importance":"The work is useful for long-context and agent-memory research because it isolates an architectural bottleneck in fixed-size textual memory. It shows that recurrent compression can lose information even when the underlying LLM is capable of extracting it. For pretraining-oriented readers, the main takeaway is that long-context behavior depends not only on context-window scaling or positional mechanisms, but also on how external memory is structured and updated.","technical_approach":"The authors decompose memory performance into Memory Capture Rate and Memory Retention Rate. Capture measures whether the gold answer ever appears in an intermediate memory state. Retention measures whether captured information survives until the final memory state. They then propose Multi-Head Recurrent Memory, a training-free framework that splits the memory budget into several independent heads. At each step, only one head is selected and updated, while all other heads remain unchanged. This reduces overwrite pressure compared with a monolithic memory block. The concrete MHM-LRU variant updates the least-recently-used head, gives uniform head utilization, and keeps the same token budget as the single-head baseline.","experiments":"On long-context benchmarks such as RULER-HQA, the diagnostic study shows that capture stays relatively stable as context length grows, while retention drops sharply. For MemAgent, retention falls below 30% at 896K tokens. MHM-LRU raises the RULER-HQA retention rate at 896K tokens to 73.96%, and the retention gain translates into more stable end-task accuracy across the 100K to 1M token range. The reported results cover multiple model families, scales, and task types, suggesting that the effect is tied to the memory architecture rather than a single model or prompt template.","reading_guide":"A 20–30 minute skim is enough.
